The agent refines the WHOLE GROUP PATTERN per iter — multi-member translation moves for spacing, and per-member yaw targets (NEVER uniform yaw deltas) for facing direction. - **stage4-schema-raw-view-reference-pattern**: In Stage-4 `json_schema.yaml`, each entity has a "raw" definition (`__&lt;model&gt;`, holding the actual `properties`, `x-bridge`, `x-display`, etc.) and a "view" definition (`&lt;model&gt;`, a thin wrapper with no properties of its own — its fields are reached only via `allOf[0].$ref` to the raw entity). Code that looks up `schema['definitions'].get(model, {})` directly (the view entity) instead of resolving to the raw entity will silently get an empty or incomplete dict, missing `properties`/`x-bridge`/`x-display`. ([curryfromuestc/curry-train](https://github.com/curryfromuestc/curry-train/tree/HEAD/skills/stage5-checkpoint-cadence))
- **stage5-loss-spike-rollback**: An automated recovery procedure for loss spikes during long-running training — detect a spike, roll back to a recent checkpoint, skip a window of batches, resume. ([curryfromuestc/curry-train](https://github.com/curryfromuestc/curry-train/tree/HEAD/skills/stage5-loss-spike-rollback))
